slates daemon: the watchdog beats every 100 ms and finds a shard stuck once it has gone its window without a step, with no CPU progress and no wait for a CPU, or has run its window of CPU, held to this build, without a step; a shard's window is the 1000 ms liveness window, raised to the scheduler overrun the shard measures where that is longer, at most 10000 ms (the observation budget), so a poll may run 4490 ms of CPU at least (this build runs compiled code 4.4 times as slowly as the machine runs it in machine code, 22900 ns against 5100 ns for a batch of 8 chains); a shard waiting for a CPU is starved, not stuck, and the beat goes on, and so is one waiting on memory while the host reports a memory stall (the low-memory resource notification, signaled: Some(Level { pressed: false }) now); an anchor reads the beat, so a stuck shard withholds it
